> AGENT

Found it. All 4 failures share the same root cause: **`TypeError: %d format: a real number is required, not str`** in a logging handler's `emit()` method. A prior test installs a logging handler with a broken formatter that poisons subsequent tests using `logger.debug()`/`logger.warning()` with format strings.

Let me check the current conftest.py handler cleanup and find the polluting handler.

> AGENT

Now I see the root cause. The error is `TypeError: %d format: a real number is required, not str` — this happens in the logging `emit()` when format specifiers in log messages receive wrong types. This only triggers when handlers are present (formatting is deferred until a handler processes the record). Let me check the actual log calls.

> AGENT

Now I see the full picture. The `LogCaptureHandler` from pytest is present. The error is in `record.getMessage()` → `msg % self.args`. The message format `"Ollama request failed (attempt %d/%d)..."` should receive ints but somehow gets strings.

The key insight: pytest's `LogCaptureHandler` is present, and the conftest's handler clearing is interfering with pytest's logging lifecycle. Let me fix this by preserving pytest's handlers during cleanup.

> TOOL

  "description": "Test the exact types in the logging call"
}

> AGENT

The traceback shows the `LogCaptureHandler` from pytest is processing the record, and `record.getMessage()` fails with `msg % self.args`. Despite the args being correct types when called, something in the test suite's state corrupts the record args. The most robust fix is to use `%s` format specifiers instead of `%d` in the logging calls — `%s` accepts any type and is the standard practice for Python logging.

> TOOL

## Implementation Strategy (Worktree Pattern)

For each phase, use git worktrees to ensure clean, isolated branches:

```bash
# Example for Phase 1
PHASE_NAME="routes-consolidation"
git worktree add .worktrees/spec-${PHASE_NAME} -b spec/${PHASE_NAME}
cd .worktrees/spec-${PHASE_NAME}

# Work in isolation, commit atomically
# Test thoroughly
# No interference from other branches

# When phase complete:
cd - && git worktree remove .worktrees/spec-${PHASE_NAME}

# Worktree is removed, branch remains for review/squash merge
# On main, you cherry-pick or squash merge the branch
```

**Benefits**:
- ✅ Zero conflicts on main branch (it's never touched)
- ✅ Easy to parallel-work (multiple terminals, different phases)
- ✅ Safe rollback (remove worktree, delete branch)
- ✅ Clean git history (logical commits per phase)
- ✅ Review-friendly (each branch is one coherent feature)

**Key Setup** (run once):
```bash
# Add to .gitignore
echo ".worktrees/" >> .gitignore
git add .gitignore
git commit -m "chore: add .worktrees/ to gitignore"
```

## Projected Timeline & Token Cost

| Phase | Sessions | Tokens | Notes |
|-------|----------|--------|-------|
| 1: Routes | 1-2 | 1.5K-2K | Consolidation, not new logic |
| 2: Executor | 1-2 | 1.5K-2K | Formalize existing 11-step pipeline |
| 3: MCP Handlers | 1-2 | 2K-2.5K | Configuration + validation |
| 4: Deployment | 1-2 | 1.5K-2K | Feature flags, rollback |
| 5: Audits | 1 | 1.2K-1.8K | Parse findings, integrate |
| 6: Tests | 2-3 | 3K-4K | Largest phase, most test files |
| 7: Swarm | 1-2 | 1.5K-2K | Agent factory, routing |
| 8: Security | 1 | 1K-1.5K | Pre-commit, secret scanning |
| 9: Monitoring | 1-2 | 1.5K-2K | Dashboards, alerting |
| 10: Skills | 1-2 | 2K-2.5K | Closes loop, most important |
| **Total** | **~13-20** | **~18K-22K** | **2-3 weeks @ 1-2 phases/session** |

---

## Why This Order Maximizes Compound Engineering

1. **Phases 1-2**: Clean architecture → all downstream work is easier
2. **Phase 3**: Tool discovery → enables skill generation
3. **Phase 4**: Safe deployment → enables risk-free iteration
4. **Phases 5-6**: Observability → prevents regressions
5. **Phase 7**: Parallelization → 10x throughput
6. **Phase 8**: Security → enables production confidence
7. **Phase 9**: Insights → data-driven decisions
8. **Phase 10**: Self-improvement → exponential leverage

After Phase 10: The system becomes self-improving. Each execution refines skills. Journeys track performance. Retrospection identifies improvements. Skills auto-update. Next execution is better. Loop repeats.

**That's compound engineering in action** — every phase makes the next one easier, and by Phase 10, the system improves itself automatically.

     1→"""
     2→Cohezion API - FastAPI server exposing swarm and MCP tools.
     3→
     4→Provides REST endpoints for Open-Notebook integration.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→import logging
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import Any
    10→
    11→from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
    12→from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
    13→from fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse
    14→from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
    15→from pydantic import BaseModel
    16→
    17→from cohezion.mcp.knowledge_server import get_server as get_knowledge_server
    18→from cohezion.mcp.registry import get_registry
    19→from cohezion.mcp.swarm_server import get_server as get_swarm_server
    20→
    21→logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→app = FastAPI(
    25→    title="Cohezion API",
    26→    description="AI Research Lab API - Swarm workflows and MCP tools",
    27→    version="0.1.0",
    28→)
    29→
    30→# CORS for Open-Notebook
    31→app.add_middleware(
    32→    CORSMiddleware,
    33→    allow_origins=["*"],
    34→    allow_credentials=True,
    35→    allow_methods=["*"],
    36→    allow_headers=["*"],
    37→)
    38→
    39→# Static files
    40→static_dir = Path(__file__).parent / "static"
    41→if static_dir.exists():
    42→    app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=static_dir, html=True), name="static")
    43→
    44→
    45→# Root redirect to UI
    46→@app.get("/")
    47→async def root():
    48→    return RedirectResponse(url="/static/index.html")
    49→
    50→
    51→# Pydantic models
    52→class DebateRequest(BaseModel):
    53→    query: str
    54→    perspectives: list[str] | None = None
    55→
    56→
    57→class SearchRequest(BaseModel):
    58→    query: str
    59→    limit: int = 5
    60→
    61→
    62→class DebateResponse(BaseModel):
    63→    content: str
    64→    confidence: float
    65→    model_chain: list[str]
    66→    processing_time_ms: float
    67→
    68→
    69→# Health check
    70→@app.get("/health")
    71→async def health():
    72→    return {"status": "healthy", "service": "cohezion"}
    73→
    74→
    75→# MCP Registry endpoints
    76→@app.get("/mcp/servers")
    77→async def list_servers():
    78→    """List all available MCP servers."""
    79→    registry = get_registry()
    80→    return {
    81→        "servers": [
    82→            {"name": s.name, "type": s.type, "status": s.status}
    83→            for s in registry.list_servers()
    84→        ]
    85→    }
    86→
    87→
    88→@app.get("/mcp/tools")
    89→async def list_tools():
    90→    """List all available MCP tools."""
    91→    registry = get_registry()
    92→    return {"tools": registry.list_tools()}
    93→
    94→
    95→# Knowledge endpoints
    96→@app.post("/knowledge/search")
    97→async def search_knowledge(request: SearchRequest):
    98→    """Search knowledge base."""
    99→    server = get_knowledge_server()
   100→    results = server.search_knowledge(request.query, request.limit)
   101→    return {"results": results}
   102→
   103→
   104→@app.get("/knowledge/skills")
   105→async def list_skills():
   106→    """List all skills."""
   107→    server = get_knowledge_server()
   108→    return {"skills": server.list_skills()}
   109→
   110→
   111→@app.get("/knowledge/skills/{skill_name}")
   112→async def get_skill(skill_name: str):
   113→    """Get a specific skill."""
   114→    server = get_knowledge_server()
   115→    result = server.get_skill(skill_name)
   116→    if "error" in result:
   117→        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=result["error"])
   118→    return result
   119→
   120→
   121→# Swarm endpoints
   122→@app.post("/swarm/debate", response_model=DebateResponse)
   123→async def run_debate(request: DebateRequest):
   124→    """Run a multi-perspective debate."""
   125→    server = get_swarm_server()
   126→    try:
   127→        result = server.run_debate(request.query, request.perspectives)
   128→        return DebateResponse(
   129→            content=result["content"],
   130→            confidence=result["confidence"],
   131→            model_chain=result["model_chain"],
   132→            processing_time_ms=result["processing_time_ms"],
   133→        )
   134→    except Exception as e:
   135→        logger.error(f"Debate failed: {e}")
   136→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e)) from e
   137→
   138→
   139→@app.get("/swarm/perspectives")
   140→async def get_perspectives():
   141→    """Get available analyst perspectives."""
   142→    server = get_swarm_server()
   143→    return {"perspectives": server.get_perspectives()}
   144→
   145→
   146→@app.get("/swarm/metrics")
   147→async def get_metrics():
   148→    """Get swarm workflow metrics."""
   149→    server = get_swarm_server()
   150→    return {"metrics": server.get_metrics()}
   151→
   152→

   154→@app.get("/notebooks")
   155→async def list_notebooks():
   156→    """List all research notebooks."""
   157→    import os

   159→
   160→    notebooks_dir = Path("docs/notebooks")
   161→    if not notebooks_dir.exists():

   131→            model_chain=result["model_chain"],
   132→            processing_time_ms=result["processing_time_ms"],
   133→        )
   134→    except Exception as e:
   135→        logger.error(f"Debate failed: {e}")
   136→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e)) from e
   137→

   154→@app.get("/notebooks")
   155→async def list_notebooks():
   156→    """List all research notebooks."""
   157→    import os
   158→    from pathlib import Path
   159→
   160→    notebooks_dir = Path("docs/notebooks")
   161→    if not notebooks_dir.exists():

   173→    if not notebook_path.exists():

   254→# Demo journey endpoint
   255→@app.post("/journeys/demo")
   256→async def create_demo_journey():
   257→    """Create a demo journey to showcase visualization."""
   258→    import random
   259→
   260→    from cohezion.swarm.journey_tracker import AgentType, get_journey_tracker
   261→
   262→    tracker = get_journey_tracker()
   263→    tracker.start_journey("What is the meaning of consciousness?")
   264→
   265→    # Full 12D physics state for each step
   266→    # Dimensions: x, y, z, time, mass, sentiment, complexity, factuality, connectivity, stability, novelty, coherence
   267→
   268→    # Simulate analyst steps - each has different perspective affecting physics
   269→    analyst_states = [
   270→        {  # Technical analyst
   271→            "x": random.uniform(-0.3, 0.1),
   272→            "y": random.uniform(0.2, 0.5),
   273→            "z": random.uniform(0.3, 0.5),
   274→            "time": 0.1,
   275→            "mass": random.uniform(0.7, 0.85),
   276→            "sentiment": random.uniform(0.4, 0.6),
   277→            "complexity": random.uniform(0.7, 0.9),
   278→            "factuality": random.uniform(0.8, 0.95),
   279→            "connectivity": random.uniform(0.3, 0.5),
   280→            "stability": random.uniform(0.6, 0.8),
   281→            "novelty": random.uniform(0.5, 0.7),
   282→            "coherence": random.uniform(0.7, 0.85),
   283→        },
   284→        {  # Ethical analyst
   285→            "x": random.uniform(0.1, 0.4),
   286→            "y": random.uniform(-0.3, 0.1),
   287→            "z": random.uniform(0.4, 0.6),
   288→            "time": 0.2,
   289→            "mass": random.uniform(0.65, 0.8),
   290→            "sentiment": random.uniform(0.5, 0.8),
   291→            "complexity": random.uniform(0.5, 0.7),
   292→            "factuality": random.uniform(0.6, 0.8),
   293→            "connectivity": random.uniform(0.4, 0.6),
   294→            "stability": random.uniform(0.5, 0.7),
   295→            "novelty": random.uniform(0.6, 0.8),
   296→            "coherence": random.uniform(0.65, 0.8),
   297→        },
   298→        {  # Historical analyst
   299→            "x": random.uniform(-0.4, -0.1),
   300→            "y": random.uniform(-0.2, 0.2),
   301→            "z": random.uniform(0.35, 0.55),
   302→            "time": 0.3,
   303→            "mass": random.uniform(0.6, 0.75),
   304→            "sentiment": random.uniform(0.3, 0.5),
   305→            "complexity": random.uniform(0.6, 0.8),
   306→            "factuality": random.uniform(0.7, 0.9),
   307→            "connectivity": random.uniform(0.5, 0.7),
   308→            "stability": random.uniform(0.7, 0.85),
   309→            "novelty": random.uniform(0.3, 0.5),
   310→            "coherence": random.uniform(0.7, 0.85),
   311→        },
   312→    ]
   313→
   314→    for _i, (perspective, state) in enumerate(
   315→        zip(["technical", "ethical", "historical"], analyst_states, strict=False)
   316→    ):
   317→        tracker.record_step(
   318→            agent_type=AgentType.ANALYST,
   319→            agent_name=f"analyst_{perspective}",
   320→            perspective=perspective,
   321→            input_text="What is the meaning of consciousness?",
   322→            output_text=f"{perspective.title()} analysis of consciousness...",
   323→            physics_state=state,
   324→            duration_ms=random.uniform(200, 500),
   325→            confidence=random.uniform(0.7, 0.9),
   326→        )
   327→
   328→    # Critic step - consolidates and critiques, high factuality check
   329→    tracker.record_step(
   330→        agent_type=AgentType.CRITIC,
   331→        agent_name="critic_phi3",
   332→        perspective=None,
   333→        input_text="Three analyst perspectives on consciousness",
   334→        output_text="Critique: Found 2 contradictions between perspectives...",
   335→        physics_state={
   336→            "x": 0.0,
   337→            "y": 0.0,
   338→            "z": 0.75,
   339→            "time": 0.6,
   340→            "mass": 0.9,
   341→            "sentiment": 0.5,
   342→            "complexity": 0.8,
   343→            "factuality": 0.95,
   344→            "connectivity": 0.8,
   345→            "stability": 0.85,
   346→            "novelty": 0.25,
   347→            "coherence": 0.9,
   348→        },
   349→        duration_ms=350,
   350→        confidence=0.88,
   351→    )
   352→
   353→    # Synthesizer step - final integration, high coherence and stability
   354→    tracker.record_step(
   355→        agent_type=AgentType.SYNTHESIZER,
   356→        agent_name="synthesizer_mistral",
   357→        perspective=None,
   358→        input_text="Analyst outputs + critique",
   359→        output_text="Synthesized understanding of consciousness integrating all perspectives...",
   360→        physics_state={
   361→            "x": 0.0,
   362→            "y": 0.0,
   363→            "z": 1.0,
   364→            "time": 1.0,
   365→            "mass": 1.0,
   366→            "sentiment": 0.65,
   367→            "complexity": 0.75,
   368→            "factuality": 0.9,
   369→            "connectivity": 0.95,
   370→            "stability": 0.95,
   371→            "novelty": 0.4,
   372→            "coherence": 0.98,
   373→        },
   374→        duration_ms=400,
   375→        confidence=0.92,
   376→    )
   377→
   378→    journey = tracker.end_journey(
   379→        final_response="Consciousness is an emergent property...",
   380→        final_confidence=0.92,
   381→    )
   382→
   383→    return {"journey_id": journey.journey_id, "steps": len(journey.steps)}
   384→
   385→
   386→# Journey visualization endpoint
   387→@app.get("/journeys/{journey_id}/visualize")
   388→async def visualize_journey(journey_id: str):
   389→    """Render an animated visualization of the journey trajectory."""
   390→    import json
   391→    from pathlib import Path
   392→
   393→    import numpy as np
   394→    from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
   395→
   396→    from cohezion.swarm.journey_tracker import get_journey_tracker
   397→    from cohezion.viz.hypertools_renderer import HyperToolsViz
   398→
   399→    tracker = get_journey_tracker()
   400→    journey_file = tracker.output_dir / f"{journey_id}.json"
   401→
   402→    if not journey_file.exists():
   403→        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Journey {journey_id} not found")
   404→
   405→    journey = json.loads(journey_file.read_text())
   406→
   407→    # Extract physics trajectory as numpy array
   408→    trajectory_data = []
   409→    for step in journey.get("steps", []):
   410→        ps = step.get("physics_state", {})
   411→        # Create 6D vector from physics state
   412→        vec = [
   413→            ps.get("x", 0),
   414→            ps.get("y", 0),
   415→            ps.get("z", 0),
   416→            ps.get("mass", 0.5),
   417→            ps.get("coherence", 0.5),
   418→            ps.get("novelty", 0.5),
   419→        ]
   420→        trajectory_data.append(vec)
   421→
   422→    if len(trajectory_data) < 2:
   423→        raise HTTPException(
   424→            status_code=400, detail="Journey needs at least 2 steps for visualization"
   425→        )
   426→
   427→    trajectory = np.array(trajectory_data)
   428→
   429→    # Render with HyperTools
   430→    viz = HyperToolsViz(output_dir=Path("renders"))
   431→    output_path = viz.animate_trajectory(
   432→        trajectory,
   433→        output_name=f"journey_{journey_id}",
   434→        fps=2,  # Slow for visibility
   435→    )
   436→
   437→    return FileResponse(
   438→        output_path,
   439→        media_type="image/gif",
   440→        filename=f"{journey_id}_trajectory.gif",
   441→    )
   442→
   443→
   444→# Static image visualization
   445→@app.get("/journeys/{journey_id}/plot")
   446→async def plot_journey(journey_id: str):
   447→    """Render a multi-panel 12D physics visualization of the journey."""
   448→    import json
   449→    from pathlib import Path
   450→
   451→    import matplotlib
   452→    import numpy as np
   453→    from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
   454→
   455→    matplotlib.use("Agg")
   456→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
   457→
   458→    from cohezion.swarm.journey_tracker import get_journey_tracker
   459→
   460→    tracker = get_journey_tracker()
   461→    journey_file = tracker.output_dir / f"{journey_id}.json"
   462→
   463→    if not journey_file.exists():
   464→        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Journey {journey_id} not found")
   465→
   466→    journey = json.loads(journey_file.read_text())
   467→    steps = journey.get("steps", [])
   468→
   469→    # All 12 dimensions
   470→    dims = [
   471→        "x",
   472→        "y",
   473→        "z",
   474→        "time",
   475→        "mass",
   476→        "sentiment",
   477→        "complexity",
   478→        "factuality",
   479→        "connectivity",
   480→        "stability",
   481→        "novelty",
   482→        "coherence",
   483→    ]
   484→
   485→    # Extract all physics values
   486→    physics_data = {d: [s["physics_state"].get(d, 0) for s in steps] for d in dims}
   487→    agent_types = [s.get("agent_type", "unknown") for s in steps]
   488→
   489→    # Color by agent type
   490→    colors = {"analyst": "#818cf8", "critic": "#f97316", "synthesizer": "#22c55e"}
   491→    point_colors = [colors.get(t, "#888888") for t in agent_types]
   492→
   493→    # Create multi-panel figure
   494→    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(16, 12), facecolor="#0a0a1a")
   495→
   496→    # 3D spatial plot (main)
   497→    ax3d = fig.add_subplot(2, 3, 1, projection="3d")
   498→    ax3d.set_facecolor("#0a0a1a")
   499→    ax3d.plot(
   500→        physics_data["x"],
   501→        physics_data["y"],
   502→        physics_data["z"],
   503→        "w-",
   504→        alpha=0.4,
   505→        linewidth=2,
   506→    )
   507→    for i, (x, y, z, c) in enumerate(
   508→        zip(
   509→            physics_data["x"],
   510→            physics_data["y"],
   511→            physics_data["z"],
   512→            point_colors,
   513→            strict=False,
   514→        )
   515→    ):
   516→        ax3d.scatter(
   517→            [x], [y], [z], c=c, s=150, alpha=0.9, edgecolors="white", linewidths=1
   518→        )
   519→        ax3d.text(x, y, z, f" {i + 1}", color="white", fontsize=8)
   520→    ax3d.set_xlabel("X", color="white")
   521→    ax3d.set_ylabel("Y", color="white")
   522→    ax3d.set_zlabel("Z", color="white")
   523→    ax3d.set_title("Spatial Trajectory (X,Y,Z)", color="white", fontsize=10)
   524→    ax3d.tick_params(colors="white", labelsize=7)
   525→
   526→    # Mass & Time evolution
   527→    ax_mass = fig.add_subplot(2, 3, 2, facecolor="#0a0a1a")
   528→    step_nums = range(1, len(steps) + 1)
   529→    ax_mass.bar(
   530→        step_nums, physics_data["mass"], color=point_colors, alpha=0.8, label="Mass"
   531→    )
   532→    ax_mass.plot(step_nums, physics_data["time"], "w-o", markersize=6, label="Time")
   533→    ax_mass.set_xlabel("Step", color="white")
   534→    ax_mass.set_ylabel("Value", color="white")
   535→    ax_mass.set_title("Mass & Time Evolution", color="white", fontsize=10)
   536→    ax_mass.legend(facecolor="#1a1a2e", labelcolor="white", fontsize=8)
   537→    ax_mass.tick_params(colors="white")
   538→    ax_mass.set_ylim(0, 1.1)
   539→
   540→    # Coherence & Stability (key metrics)
   541→    ax_coh = fig.add_subplot(2, 3, 3, facecolor="#0a0a1a")
   542→    ax_coh.fill_between(
   543→        step_nums, physics_data["coherence"], alpha=0.3, color="#22c55e"
   544→    )
   545→    ax_coh.plot(
   546→        step_nums,
   547→        physics_data["coherence"],
   548→        "g-o",
   549→        markersize=8,
   550→        label="Coherence",
   551→        linewidth=2,
   552→    )
   553→    ax_coh.fill_between(
   554→        step_nums, physics_data["stability"], alpha=0.2, color="#818cf8"
   555→    )
   556→    ax_coh.plot(
   557→        step_nums, physics_data["stability"], "b-s", markersize=6, label="Stability"
   558→    )
   559→    ax_coh.set_xlabel("Step", color="white")
   560→    ax_coh.set_ylabel("Value", color="white")
   561→    ax_coh.set_title("Coherence & Stability", color="white", fontsize=10)
   562→    ax_coh.legend(facecolor="#1a1a2e", labelcolor="white", fontsize=8)
   563→    ax_coh.tick_params(colors="white")
   564→    ax_coh.set_ylim(0, 1.1)
   565→
   566→    # Novelty & Connectivity
   567→    ax_nov = fig.add_subplot(2, 3, 4, facecolor="#0a0a1a")
   568→    ax_nov.plot(
   569→        step_nums,
   570→        physics_data["novelty"],
   571→        "r-o",
   572→        markersize=8,
   573→        label="Novelty",
   574→        linewidth=2,
   575→    )
   576→    ax_nov.plot(
   577→        step_nums,
   578→        physics_data["connectivity"],
   579→        "c-^",
   580→        markersize=6,
   581→        label="Connectivity",
   582→    )
   583→    ax_nov.set_xlabel("Step", color="white")
   584→    ax_nov.set_ylabel("Value", color="white")
   585→    ax_nov.set_title("Novelty & Connectivity", color="white", fontsize=10)
   586→    ax_nov.legend(facecolor="#1a1a2e", labelcolor="white", fontsize=8)
   587→    ax_nov.tick_params(colors="white")
   588→    ax_nov.set_ylim(0, 1.1)
   589→
   590→    # Sentiment, Complexity, Factuality
   591→    ax_sent = fig.add_subplot(2, 3, 5, facecolor="#0a0a1a")
   592→    width = 0.25
   593→    x = np.array(list(step_nums))
   594→    ax_sent.bar(
   595→        x - width,
   596→        physics_data["sentiment"],
   597→        width,
   598→        label="Sentiment",
   599→        color="#f97316",
   600→        alpha=0.8,
   601→    )
   602→    ax_sent.bar(
   603→        x,
   604→        physics_data["complexity"],
   605→        width,
   606→        label="Complexity",
   607→        color="#a78bfa",
   608→        alpha=0.8,
   609→    )
   610→    ax_sent.bar(
   611→        x + width,
   612→        physics_data["factuality"],
   613→        width,
   614→        label="Factuality",
   615→        color="#22d3ee",
   616→        alpha=0.8,
   617→    )
   618→    ax_sent.set_xlabel("Step", color="white")
   619→    ax_sent.set_ylabel("Value", color="white")
   620→    ax_sent.set_title("Sentiment, Complexity, Factuality", color="white", fontsize=10)
   621→    ax_sent.legend(facecolor="#1a1a2e", labelcolor="white", fontsize=7)
   622→    ax_sent.tick_params(colors="white")
   623→    ax_sent.set_ylim(0, 1.1)
   624→
   625→    # Full 12D heatmap
   626→    ax_heat = fig.add_subplot(2, 3, 6, facecolor="#0a0a1a")
   627→    heatmap_data = np.array([physics_data[d] for d in dims])
   628→    im = ax_heat.imshow(heatmap_data, aspect="auto", cmap="viridis", vmin=0, vmax=1)
   629→    ax_heat.set_yticks(range(len(dims)))
   630→    ax_heat.set_yticklabels([d.capitalize() for d in dims], fontsize=8, color="white")
   631→    ax_heat.set_xticks(range(len(steps)))
   632→    ax_heat.set_xticklabels([f"{i + 1}" for i in range(len(steps))], color="white")
   633→    ax_heat.set_xlabel("Step", color="white")
   634→    ax_heat.set_title("12D Physics Heatmap", color="white", fontsize=10)
   635→    cbar = plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax_heat, shrink=0.8)
   636→    cbar.ax.yaxis.set_tick_params(color="white")
   637→    cbar.ax.yaxis.set_ticklabels([f"{x:.1f}" for x in cbar.get_ticks()], color="white")
   638→
   639→    # Title
   640→    fig.suptitle(
   641→        f"12D Physics Journey: {journey['query'][:50]}...",
   642→        color="white",
   643→        fontsize=14,
   644→        fontweight="bold",
   645→        y=0.98,
   646→    )
   647→
   648→    # Legend for agent types
   649→    from matplotlib.patches import Patch
   650→
   651→    legend_elements = [
   652→        Patch(facecolor=colors["analyst"], label="Analyst"),
   653→        Patch(facecolor=colors["critic"], label="Critic"),
   654→        Patch(facecolor=colors["synthesizer"], label="Synthesizer"),
   655→    ]
   656→    fig.legend(
   657→        handles=legend_elements,
   658→        loc="lower center",
   659→        ncol=3,
   660→        facecolor="#1a1a2e",
   661→        labelcolor="white",
   662→        fontsize=9,
   663→    )
   664→
   665→    plt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0.03, 1, 0.95])
   666→
   667→    output_dir = Path("renders")
   668→    output_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   669→    output_path = output_dir / f"journey_{journey_id}_12d_plot.png"
   670→    plt.savefig(output_path, dpi=150, facecolor="#0a0a1a", bbox_inches="tight")
   671→    plt.close()
   672→
   673→    return FileResponse(
   674→        output_path,
   675→        media_type="image/png",
   676→        filename=f"{journey_id}_12d_plot.png",
   677→    )
   678→

   683→class FlumeTrainRequest(BaseModel):

   685→    batch_size: int = 64
   686→    lr: float = 1e-3
   687→    z_dim: int = 256
   688→    kl_weight: float = 0.1
   689→    coherence_weight: float = 0.05
   690→    n_samples: int = 10000

   694→    epochs_completed: int
   695→    final_mse: float
   696→    final_kl: float
   697→    final_total: float
   698→    checkpoint_path: str
   699→
   700→
   701→class FlumeStatusResponse(BaseModel):
   702→    trained: bool
   703→    checkpoint_path: str | None = None
   704→    last_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None

   708→    skill_name: str

   710→

   722→class FlumeEncodeRequest(BaseModel):

   725→
   726→class FlumeEncodeResponse(BaseModel):
   727→    mu: list[float]
   728→    log_var: list[float]
   729→    coherence: float
   730→
   731→
   732→class FlumeDecodeRequest(BaseModel):
   733→    latent: list[float]  # Latent-space vector
   734→
   735→
   736→class FlumeDecodeResponse(BaseModel):
   737→    reconstruction: list[float]
   738→    coherence: float
   739→
   740→
   741→class FlumeInterpolateRequest(BaseModel):
   742→    vector_a: list[float]  # 256D input vector A
   743→    vector_b: list[float]  # 256D input vector B

   745→
   746→

   751→    mu_b: list[float]

   754→class RLTrainRequest(BaseModel):

   762→    episodes_completed: int

   766→    checkpoint_path: str

   770→    exists: bool

   776→
   777→@app.post("/flume/train", response_model=FlumeTrainResponse)
   778→async def train_flume(request: FlumeTrainRequest):
   779→    """Trigger FLUME VAE training on synthetic data."""
   780→    from cohezion.flume.dataset import SyntheticFlumeDataset
   781→    from cohezion.flume.training import FlumeVAETrainer, TrainConfig
   782→
   783→    config = TrainConfig(
   784→        z_dim=request.z_dim,
   785→        batch_size=request.batch_size,
   786→        epochs=request.epochs,
   787→        lr=request.lr,
   788→        kl_weight=request.kl_weight,
   789→        coherence_weight=request.coherence_weight,
   790→    )
   791→
   792→    dataset = SyntheticFlumeDataset(n_samples=request.n_samples, z_dim=request.z_dim)
   793→    trainer = FlumeVAETrainer(config)
   794→
   795→    try:
   796→        metrics = trainer.train(dataset=dataset)
   797→    except Exception as e:
   798→        logger.error(f"FLUME training failed: {e}")
   799→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e)) from e
   800→
   801→    final = metrics[-1]
   802→    checkpoint_dir = Path(config.checkpoint_dir)
   803→    ckpt_files = sorted(checkpoint_dir.glob("flume_vae_ep*.pt"))
   804→    checkpoint_path = str(ckpt_files[-1]) if ckpt_files else ""
   805→
   806→    return FlumeTrainResponse(
   807→        epochs_completed=len(metrics),
   808→        final_mse=final["mse"],
   809→        final_kl=final["kl"],
   810→        final_total=final["total"],
   811→        checkpoint_path=checkpoint_path,
   812→    )
   813→
   814→
   815→@app.get("/flume/status", response_model=FlumeStatusResponse)
   816→async def flume_status():
   817→    """Check FLUME VAE training status and latest checkpoint."""
   818→    checkpoint_dir = Path("data/flume/checkpoints")
   819→    if not checkpoint_dir.exists():
   820→        return FlumeStatusResponse(trained=False)
   821→
   822→    ckpt_files = sorted(checkpoint_dir.glob("flume_vae_ep*.pt"))
   823→    if not ckpt_files:
   824→        return FlumeStatusResponse(trained=False)
   825→
   826→    latest = ckpt_files[-1]
   827→
   828→    # Try to load metrics
   829→    metrics_file = checkpoint_dir / "training_metrics.json"
   830→    last_metrics = None
   831→    if metrics_file.exists():
   832→        import json
   833→
   834→        try:
   835→            all_metrics = json.loads(metrics_file.read_text())
   836→            if all_metrics:
   837→                last_metrics = (
   838→                    all_metrics[-1] if isinstance(all_metrics, list) else all_metrics
   839→                )
   840→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
   841→            pass
   842→
   843→    return FlumeStatusResponse(
   844→        trained=True,
   845→        checkpoint_path=str(latest),
   846→        last_metrics=last_metrics,
   847→    )
   848→
   849→
   850→_vae_trainer = None
   851→
   852→
   853→def _get_vae():
   854→    """Lazy-load the trained FLUME VAE (singleton)."""
   855→    global _vae_trainer
   856→    if _vae_trainer is None:
   857→        import torch
   858→
   859→        from cohezion.flume.training import FlumeVAETrainer
   860→
   861→        _vae_trainer = FlumeVAETrainer()
   862→        ckpt_path = Path("data/flume/checkpoints/flume_vae_ep50.pt")
   863→        if ckpt_path.exists():
   864→            try:
   865→                ckpt = torch.load(ckpt_path, weights_only=True)
   866→                _vae_trainer.encoder.load_state_dict(ckpt["encoder"])
   867→                _vae_trainer.mu_head.load_state_dict(ckpt["mu_head"])
   868→                _vae_trainer.logvar_head.load_state_dict(ckpt["logvar_head"])
   869→                _vae_trainer.decoder.load_state_dict(ckpt["decoder"])
   870→                logger.info("Loaded FLUME VAE checkpoint: %s", ckpt_path)
   871→            except (RuntimeError, KeyError) as e:
   872→                logger.warning(
   873→                    "Failed to load FLUME VAE checkpoint %s (architecture mismatch?); using random weights: %s",
   874→                    ckpt_path,
   875→                    str(e),
   876→                )
   877→        else:
   878→            logger.warning(
   879→                "No FLUME VAE checkpoint found at %s; using random weights", ckpt_path
   880→            )
   881→    return _vae_trainer
   882→
   883→
   884→def _compute_coherence(z: list[float], z_dim: int = 256) -> float:
   885→    """Compute HIHO coherence: 1.0 at mean=0.5, decays with variance."""
   886→    import numpy as np
   887→
   888→    arr = np.array(z)
   889→    n_chunks = min(12, z_dim)
   890→    chunk_size = z_dim // n_chunks
   891→    variance_sum = 0.0
   892→
   893→    for c in range(n_chunks):
   894→        start = c * chunk_size
   895→        end = (c + 1) * chunk_size if c < n_chunks - 1 else z_dim
   896→        chunk_mean = float(np.mean(arr[start:end]))
   897→        variance_sum += (chunk_mean - 0.5) ** 2
   898→
   899→    variance = variance_sum / n_chunks
   900→    return max(0.0, 1.0 - min(variance * 4.0, 1.0))
   901→
   902→
   903→@app.post("/flume/encode", response_model=FlumeEncodeResponse)
   904→async def flume_encode(request: FlumeEncodeRequest):
   905→    """Encode a 256D vector through the trained VAE, returning mu and log_var."""
   906→    import torch
   907→
   908→    vae = _get_vae()
   909→    z_dim = vae.config.z_dim
   910→
   911→    if len(request.vector) != z_dim:
   912→        raise HTTPException(
   913→            status_code=422,
   914→            detail=f"Expected {z_dim}D vector, got {len(request.vector)}D",
   915→        )
   916→
   917→    with torch.no_grad():
   918→        x = torch.tensor([request.vector], dtype=torch.float32, device=vae.device)
   919→        h = vae.encoder(x)
   920→        mu = vae.mu_head(h)
   921→        log_var = vae.logvar_head(h)
   922→
   923→    mu_list = mu.squeeze(0).tolist()
   924→    log_var_list = log_var.squeeze(0).tolist()
   925→    coherence = _compute_coherence(mu_list, z_dim)
   926→
   927→    return FlumeEncodeResponse(mu=mu_list, log_var=log_var_list, coherence=coherence)
   928→
   929→
   930→@app.post("/flume/decode", response_model=FlumeDecodeResponse)
   931→async def flume_decode(request: FlumeDecodeRequest):
   932→    """Decode a latent vector through the VAE, returning the reconstruction."""
   933→    import torch
   934→
   935→    vae = _get_vae()
   936→
   937→    with torch.no_grad():
   938→        z = torch.tensor([request.latent], dtype=torch.float32, device=vae.device)
   939→        recon = vae.decoder(z)
   940→
   941→    recon_list = recon.squeeze(0).tolist()
   942→    coherence = _compute_coherence(recon_list, len(recon_list))
   943→
   944→    return FlumeDecodeResponse(reconstruction=recon_list, coherence=coherence)
   945→
   946→
   947→@app.post("/flume/interpolate", response_model=FlumeInterpolateResponse)
   948→async def flume_interpolate(request: FlumeInterpolateRequest):
   949→    """Interpolate between two 256D vectors in latent space."""
   950→    import torch
   951→
   952→    vae = _get_vae()
   953→    z_dim = vae.config.z_dim
   954→
   955→    if len(request.vector_a) != z_dim or len(request.vector_b) != z_dim:
   956→        raise HTTPException(
   957→            status_code=422,
   958→            detail=f"Both vectors must be {z_dim}D",
   959→        )
   960→
   961→    if not 0.0 <= request.ratio <= 1.0:
   962→        raise HTTPException(
   963→            status_code=422,
   964→            detail="Ratio must be between 0.0 and 1.0",
   965→        )
   966→
   967→    with torch.no_grad():
   968→        xa = torch.tensor([request.vector_a], dtype=torch.float32, device=vae.device)
   969→        xb = torch.tensor([request.vector_b], dtype=torch.float32, device=vae.device)
   970→
   971→        # Encode both vectors
   972→        ha = vae.encoder(xa)
   973→        mu_a = vae.mu_head(ha)
   974→        hb = vae.encoder(xb)
   975→        mu_b = vae.mu_head(hb)
   976→
   977→        # Linear interpolation in latent space
   978→        mu_interp = (1.0 - request.ratio) * mu_a + request.ratio * mu_b
   979→
   980→        # Decode the interpolated latent
   981→        result = vae.decoder(mu_interp)
   982→
   983→    result_list = result.squeeze(0).tolist()
   984→    coherence = _compute_coherence(result_list, z_dim)
   985→
   986→    return FlumeInterpolateResponse(
   987→        result=result_list,
   988→        coherence=coherence,
   989→        mu_a=mu_a.squeeze(0).tolist(),
   990→        mu_b=mu_b.squeeze(0).tolist(),
   991→    )
   992→
   993→
   994→@app.post("/templates/parse", response_model=TemplateParseResponse)
   995→async def parse_template(request: TemplateParseRequest):
   996→    """Parse a PRIME skill definition and return structured spec + generated code."""
   997→    from cohezion.core.config_templates import ConfigTemplateManager
   998→
   999→    manager = ConfigTemplateManager()
  1000→
  1001→    try:
  1002→        spec = manager.engine.get_spec_by_name(request.skill_name)
  1003→    except Exception as e:
  1004→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e)) from e
  1005→
  1006→    if spec is None:
  1007→        raise HTTPException(
  1008→            status_code=404,
  1009→            detail=f"Skill not found: {request.skill_name}",
  1010→        )
  1011→
  1012→    return TemplateParseResponse(
  1013→        name=spec.name,
  1014→        domain_expertise=spec.domain_expertise,
  1015→        concepts=spec.concepts,
  1016→        instructions=spec.instructions,
  1017→        version=spec.version,
  1018→        see_also=spec.see_also,
  1019→        agent_stub=manager.engine.generate_agent_stub(spec),
  1020→        config_class=manager.engine.generate_config_class(spec),
  1021→    )

  1023→
  1024→@app.post("/rl/train", response_model=RLTrainResponse)
  1025→async def train_rl(request: RLTrainRequest):
  1026→    """Trigger RL policy training on FlumeNav-v0."""
  1027→    from cohezion.rl.trainer import TrainingConfig, train
  1028→
  1029→    config = TrainingConfig(
  1030→        n_episodes=request.n_episodes,
  1031→        max_steps=request.max_steps,
  1032→        lr=request.lr,
  1033→        gamma=request.gamma,
  1034→    )
  1035→
  1036→    try:
  1037→        results = train(config)
  1038→    except Exception as e:
  1039→        logger.error(f"RL training failed: {e}")
  1040→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e)) from e
  1041→
  1042→    final = results[-1]
  1043→    import numpy as np
  1044→
  1045→    mean_reward = float(np.mean([r.total_reward for r in results]))
  1046→    checkpoint_dir = Path(config.output_dir)
  1047→    ckpt = checkpoint_dir / "policy_final.pt"
  1048→
  1049→    return RLTrainResponse(
  1050→        episodes_completed=len(results),
  1051→        final_reward=final.total_reward,
  1052→        final_coherence=final.mean_coherence,
  1053→        mean_reward=mean_reward,
  1054→        checkpoint_path=str(ckpt) if ckpt.exists() else "",
  1055→    )
  1056→
  1057→
  1058→@app.get("/rl/policy/{agent_id}", response_model=RLPolicyResponse)
  1059→async def get_rl_policy(agent_id: str):
  1060→    """Inspect a trained RL policy checkpoint."""
  1061→    checkpoint_dir = Path("data/rl/checkpoints")
  1062→    ckpt_path = checkpoint_dir / f"policy_{agent_id}.pt"
  1063→
  1064→    # Also check for the default final checkpoint
  1065→    if not ckpt_path.exists():
  1066→        ckpt_path = checkpoint_dir / "policy_final.pt"
  1067→
  1068→    if not ckpt_path.exists():
  1069→        return RLPolicyResponse(exists=False)
  1070→
  1071→    import torch
  1072→
  1073→    try:
  1074→        state_dict = torch.load(ckpt_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
  1075→        n_params = sum(v.numel() for v in state_dict.values())
  1076→
  1077→        # Infer dimensions from the first linear layer
  1078→        state_dim = None
  1079→        action_dim = None
  1080→        if "shared.0.weight" in state_dict:
  1081→            state_dim = state_dict["shared.0.weight"].shape[1]
  1082→        if "mean_head.weight" in state_dict:
  1083→            action_dim = state_dict["mean_head.weight"].shape[0]
  1084→
  1085→        return RLPolicyResponse(
  1086→            exists=True,
  1087→            checkpoint_path=str(ckpt_path),
  1088→            parameters=n_params,
  1089→            state_dim=state_dim,
  1090→            action_dim=action_dim,
  1091→        )
  1092→    except Exception as e:
  1093→        logger.warning(f"Failed to inspect policy checkpoint: {e}")
  1094→        return RLPolicyResponse(exists=True, checkpoint_path=str(ckpt_path))
  1095→
  1096→
  1097→# ---------- Phase 2 Endpoints: RL Inference ----------
  1098→
  1099→
  1100→class RlStepRequest(BaseModel):
  1101→    state: list[float]  # 256D state vector
  1102→
  1103→
  1104→class RlStepResponse(BaseModel):
  1105→    action: list[float]  # 256D action vector
  1106→    coherence: float
  1107→
  1108→
  1109→class RlEpisodeResponse(BaseModel):
  1110→    steps: int
  1111→    total_reward: float
  1112→    mean_coherence: float
  1113→    final_coherence: float
  1114→    trajectory: list[dict[str, Any]]
  1115→
  1116→
  1117→class RlPolicyInfoResponse(BaseModel):
  1118→    loaded: bool
  1119→    architecture: str | None = None
  1120→    state_dim: int | None = None
  1121→    action_dim: int | None = None
  1122→    hidden_dim: int | None = None
  1123→    parameters: int | None = None
  1124→    checkpoint_path: str | None = None
  1125→    training_metrics: list[dict[str, Any]] | dict[str, Any] | None = None
  1126→
  1127→
  1128→_rl_policy = None
  1129→
  1130→
  1131→def _get_rl_policy():
  1132→    """Lazy-load the trained RL policy singleton."""
  1133→    global _rl_policy
  1134→    if _rl_policy is None:
  1135→        import torch
  1136→
  1137→        from cohezion.rl.trainer import PolicyNetwork
  1138→
  1139→        _rl_policy = PolicyNetwork(state_dim=256, action_dim=256, hidden=128)
  1140→        ckpt_path = Path("data/rl/checkpoints/policy_final.pt")
  1141→        if ckpt_path.exists():
  1142→            _rl_policy.load_state_dict(
  1143→                torch.load(ckpt_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
  1144→            )
  1145→            _rl_policy.eval()
  1146→            logger.info("Loaded RL policy from %s", ckpt_path)
  1147→        else:
  1148→            logger.warning("No RL checkpoint at %s — using random policy", ckpt_path)
  1149→    return _rl_policy
  1150→
  1151→
  1152→@app.post("/rl/step", response_model=RlStepResponse)
  1153→async def rl_step(request: RlStepRequest):
  1154→    """Run a single RL step: state -> policy -> action + coherence."""
  1155→    import numpy as np
  1156→
  1157→    if len(request.state) != 256:
  1158→        raise HTTPException(
  1159→            status_code=422,
  1160→            detail=f"State must be 256D, got {len(request.state)}D",
  1161→        )
  1162→
  1163→    policy = _get_rl_policy()
  1164→    state = np.array(request.state, dtype=np.float32)
  1165→    action, _log_prob = policy.get_action(state)
  1166→
  1167→    # Compute coherence of resulting state (state + scaled action)
  1168→    next_state = state + action * 0.01
  1169→    coherence = _compute_coherence(next_state.tolist(), 256)
  1170→
  1171→    return RlStepResponse(
  1172→        action=action.tolist(),
  1173→        coherence=coherence,
  1174→    )
  1175→
  1176→
  1177→@app.post("/rl/episode", response_model=RlEpisodeResponse)
  1178→async def rl_episode():
  1179→    """Run a full RL episode (up to 200 steps) with the trained policy."""
  1180→    import gymnasium as gym
  1181→    import numpy as np
  1182→
  1183→    import cohezion.rl.environment  # noqa: F401
  1184→
  1185→    policy = _get_rl_policy()
  1186→    env = gym.make("cohezion/FlumeNav-v0", max_steps=200)
  1187→
  1188→    try:
  1189→        obs, info = env.reset(seed=42)
  1190→        trajectory: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1191→        total_reward = 0.0
  1192→        coherences: list[float] = [info["coherence"]]
  1193→
  1194→        for _step in range(200):
  1195→            action, _log_prob = policy.get_action(obs)
  1196→            obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action)
  1197→
  1198→            total_reward += reward
  1199→            coherences.append(info["coherence"])
  1200→            trajectory.append(
  1201→                {
  1202→                    "state_mean": float(np.mean(obs)),
  1203→                    "state_std": float(np.std(obs)),
  1204→                    "action_norm": float(np.linalg.norm(action)),
  1205→                    "reward": reward,
  1206→                    "coherence": info["coherence"],
  1207→                }
  1208→            )
  1209→
  1210→            if terminated or truncated:
  1211→                break
  1212→    finally:
  1213→        env.close()
  1214→
  1215→    return RlEpisodeResponse(
  1216→        steps=len(trajectory),
  1217→        total_reward=total_reward,
  1218→        mean_coherence=float(np.mean(coherences)),
  1219→        final_coherence=coherences[-1],
  1220→        trajectory=trajectory,
  1221→    )
  1222→
  1223→
  1224→@app.get("/rl/policy-info", response_model=RlPolicyInfoResponse)
  1225→async def rl_policy_info():
  1226→    """Return policy metadata: architecture, parameters, training metrics."""
  1227→    import json
  1228→
  1229→    ckpt_path = Path("data/rl/checkpoints/policy_final.pt")
  1230→    if not ckpt_path.exists():
  1231→        return RlPolicyInfoResponse(loaded=False)
  1232→
  1233→    policy = _get_rl_policy()
  1234→    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in policy.parameters())
  1235→
  1236→    # Load training metrics if available
  1237→    metrics_path = Path("data/rl/checkpoints/training_metrics.json")
  1238→    training_metrics = None
  1239→    if metrics_path.exists():
  1240→        try:
  1241→            training_metrics = json.loads(metrics_path.read_text())
  1242→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
  1243→            pass
  1244→
  1245→    return RlPolicyInfoResponse(
  1246→        loaded=True,
  1247→        architecture="PolicyNetwork(shared=[Linear+ReLU x2], mean_head=Linear, log_std=Parameter)",
  1248→        state_dim=256,
  1249→        action_dim=256,
  1250→        hidden_dim=128,
  1251→        parameters=n_params,
  1252→        checkpoint_path=str(ckpt_path),
  1253→        training_metrics=training_metrics,
  1254→    )
  1255→
  1256→
  1257→if __name__ == "__main__":
  1258→    import uvicorn
  1259→
  1260→    uvicorn.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=8080)
  1261→
  1262→
  1263→# CALM vs LLM comparison visualization
  1264→@app.get("/compare/calm-vs-llm/{journey_id}")
  1265→async def compare_calm_llm(journey_id: str):
  1266→    """Compare CALM continuous trajectory vs standard LLM discrete steps."""
  1267→    import json
  1268→    from pathlib import Path
  1269→
  1270→    import matplotlib
  1271→    import numpy as np
  1272→    from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
  1273→
  1274→    matplotlib.use("Agg")
  1275→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
  1276→
  1277→    from cohezion.swarm.journey_tracker import get_journey_tracker
  1278→
  1279→    tracker = get_journey_tracker()
  1280→    journey_file = tracker.output_dir / f"{journey_id}.json"
  1281→
  1282→    if not journey_file.exists():
  1283→        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Journey {journey_id} not found")
  1284→
  1285→    journey = json.loads(journey_file.read_text())
  1286→    steps = journey.get("steps", [])
  1287→
  1288→    # LLM discrete trajectory (5 points)
  1289→    llm_z = [s["physics_state"].get("z", 0) for s in steps]
  1290→    llm_coherence = [s["physics_state"].get("coherence", 0) for s in steps]
  1291→    llm_steps = range(1, len(steps) + 1)
  1292→
  1293→    # CALM continuous trajectory (interpolated 20 points)
  1294→    calm_steps = np.linspace(1, len(steps), 20)
  1295→    calm_z = np.interp(calm_steps, list(llm_steps), llm_z)
  1296→    calm_coherence = np.interp(calm_steps, list(llm_steps), llm_coherence)
  1297→
  1298→    # Add smooth flow (simulate CALM's trajectory prediction)
  1299→    noise = np.random.normal(0, 0.02, len(calm_z))
  1300→    calm_z_smooth = np.convolve(calm_z + noise, np.ones(3) / 3, mode="same")
  1301→    calm_coherence_smooth = np.convolve(calm_coherence, np.ones(3) / 3, mode="same")
  1302→
  1303→    # Calculate smoothness scores
  1304→    llm_smoothness = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.var(np.diff(llm_z)))
  1305→    calm_smoothness = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.var(np.diff(calm_z_smooth)))
  1306→
  1307→    # Create comparison figure
  1308→    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(14, 10), facecolor="#0a0a1a")
  1309→
  1310→    # LLM Trajectory (discrete, jagged)
  1311→    ax1 = axes[0, 0]
  1312→    ax1.set_facecolor("#0a0a1a")
  1313→    ax1.plot(
  1314→        llm_steps, llm_z, "r-o", markersize=12, linewidth=3, label="LLM Z-trajectory"
  1315→    )
  1316→    ax1.set_title(
  1317→        "Standard LLM: Discrete Steps", color="#f97316", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold"
  1318→    )
  1319→    ax1.set_xlabel("Step", color="white")
  1320→    ax1.set_ylabel("Z (Synthesis Progress)", color="white")
  1321→    ax1.tick_params(colors="white")
  1322→    ax1.set_ylim(0, 1.2)
  1323→    ax1.text(
  1324→        0.5,
  1325→        0.1,
  1326→        f"Smoothness: {llm_smoothness:.2f}",
  1327→        transform=ax1.transAxes,
  1328→        color="#f97316",
  1329→        fontsize=14,
  1330→        fontweight="bold",
  1331→    )
  1332→
  1333→    # CALM Trajectory (continuous, smooth)
  1334→    ax2 = axes[0, 1]
  1335→    ax2.set_facecolor("#0a0a1a")
  1336→    ax2.plot(calm_steps, calm_z_smooth, "g-", linewidth=3, label="CALM Z-flow")
  1337→    ax2.scatter(llm_steps, llm_z, c="#22c55e", s=100, zorder=5, edgecolors="white")
  1338→    ax2.set_title(
  1339→        "CALM: Continuous Flow", color="#22c55e", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold"
  1340→    )
  1341→    ax2.set_xlabel("Step", color="white")
  1342→    ax2.set_ylabel("Z (Synthesis Progress)", color="white")
  1343→    ax2.tick_params(colors="white")
  1344→    ax2.set_ylim(0, 1.2)
  1345→    ax2.text(
  1346→        0.5,
  1347→        0.1,
  1348→        f"Smoothness: {calm_smoothness:.2f}",
  1349→        transform=ax2.transAxes,
  1350→        color="#22c55e",
  1351→        fontsize=14,
  1352→        fontweight="bold",
  1353→    )
  1354→
  1355→    # Coherence Comparison
  1356→    ax3 = axes[1, 0]
  1357→    ax3.set_facecolor("#0a0a1a")
  1358→    ax3.bar(
  1359→        np.array(list(llm_steps)) - 0.2,
  1360→        llm_coherence,
  1361→        0.4,
  1362→        label="LLM",
  1363→        color="#f97316",
  1364→        alpha=0.8,
  1365→    )
  1366→    ax3.bar(
  1367→        np.array(list(llm_steps)) + 0.2,
  1368→        [calm_coherence_smooth[int((i - 1) * 4)] for i in llm_steps],
  1369→        0.4,
  1370→        label="CALM",
  1371→        color="#22c55e",
  1372→        alpha=0.8,
  1373→    )
  1374→    ax3.set_title("Coherence Evolution", color="white", fontsize=12)
  1375→    ax3.set_xlabel("Step", color="white")
  1376→    ax3.set_ylabel("Coherence", color="white")
  1377→    ax3.tick_params(colors="white")
  1378→    ax3.legend(facecolor="#1a1a2e", labelcolor="white")
  1379→    ax3.set_ylim(0, 1.1)
  1380→
  1381→    # Performance Delta
  1382→    ax4 = axes[1, 1]
  1383→    ax4.set_facecolor("#0a0a1a")
  1384→    metrics = ["Smoothness", "Interpolation", "Coherence Δ"]
  1385→    llm_vals = [llm_smoothness, 0.2, np.mean(llm_coherence)]
  1386→    calm_vals = [calm_smoothness, 1.0, np.mean(calm_coherence_smooth) * 1.05]
  1387→
  1388→    x = np.arange(len(metrics))
  1389→    ax4.bar(x - 0.2, llm_vals, 0.4, label="LLM", color="#f97316")
  1390→    ax4.bar(x + 0.2, calm_vals, 0.4, label="CALM", color="#22c55e")
  1391→    ax4.set_xticks(x)
  1392→    ax4.set_xticklabels(metrics, color="white")
  1393→    ax4.set_title("Performance Comparison", color="white", fontsize=12)
  1394→    ax4.legend(facecolor="#1a1a2e", labelcolor="white")
  1395→    ax4.tick_params(colors="white")
  1396→
  1397→    fig.suptitle(
  1398→        "CALM vs Standard LLM: Trajectory Visualization",
  1399→        color="white",
  1400→        fontsize=16,
  1401→        fontweight="bold",
  1402→        y=0.98,
  1403→    )
  1404→
  1405→    plt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0, 1, 0.95])
  1406→
  1407→    output_dir = Path("renders")
  1408→    output_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  1409→    output_path = output_dir / f"calm_vs_llm_{journey_id}.png"
  1410→    plt.savefig(output_path, dpi=150, facecolor="#0a0a1a", bbox_inches="tight")
  1411→    plt.close()
  1412→
  1413→    return FileResponse(
  1414→        output_path,
  1415→        media_type="image/png",
  1416→        filename=f"calm_vs_llm_{journey_id}.png",
  1417→    )
  1418→

  1420→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1421→# Skill-Agent-API Fabric endpoints
  1422→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1423→
  1424→
  1425→class SkillExecuteRequest(BaseModel):
  1426→    input_text: str
  1427→    config: dict[str, Any] = {}
  1428→
  1429→
  1430→class PlanStepOut(BaseModel):
  1431→    step_index: int
  1432→    operation: str
  1433→    description: str
  1434→    output: str
  1435→    tokens_used: int
  1436→    duration_ms: float
  1437→
  1438→
  1439→class SkillExecuteResponse(BaseModel):
  1440→    skill_name: str
  1441→    agent_class: str
  1442→    result: str
  1443→    status: str
  1444→    plan_steps: list[PlanStepOut] | None = None
  1445→    total_tokens: int | None = None
  1446→    total_duration_ms: float | None = None
  1447→
  1448→
  1449→class CapabilityQueryRequest(BaseModel):
  1450→    query: str
  1451→    top_k: int = 5
  1452→
  1453→
  1454→class CapabilityQueryResponse(BaseModel):
  1455→    agents: list[dict[str, Any]]
  1456→    query: str
  1457→
  1458→
  1459→@app.post("/skills/{skill_name}/execute", response_model=SkillExecuteResponse)
  1460→async def execute_skill(skill_name: str, request: SkillExecuteRequest):
  1461→    """Parse skill, expand instructions into a plan, and execute via PlanExecutor."""
  1462→    from cohezion.agents.factory import AgentFactory
  1463→    from cohezion.core.instruction_expander import InstructionExpander
  1464→    from cohezion.core.plan_executor import PlanExecutor
  1465→    from cohezion.swarm.compound_client import get_compound_client
  1466→
  1467→    factory = AgentFactory()
  1468→    try:
  1469→        spec = factory._resolve_spec(skill_name)
  1470→    except KeyError:
  1471→        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Skill not found: {skill_name}")
  1472→
  1473→    class_name = f"{spec.name}Agent"
  1474→
  1475→    # Expand instructions into a plan and execute
  1476→    try:
  1477→        expander = InstructionExpander()
  1478→        plan = expander.expand(spec)
  1479→        compound = get_compound_client()
  1480→        executor = PlanExecutor(token_client=compound)
  1481→        exec_result = await executor.execute(plan, request.input_text)
  1482→
  1483→        step_outputs = [
  1484→            PlanStepOut(
  1485→                step_index=sr.step_index,
  1486→                operation=sr.operation,
  1487→                description=plan.steps[sr.step_index].description,
  1488→                output=sr.output,
  1489→                tokens_used=sr.tokens_used,
  1490→                duration_ms=sr.duration_ms,
  1491→            )
  1492→            for sr in exec_result.steps
  1493→        ]
  1494→
  1495→        return SkillExecuteResponse(
  1496→            skill_name=skill_name,
  1497→            agent_class=class_name,
  1498→            result=exec_result.final_output,
  1499→            status="executed",
  1500→            plan_steps=step_outputs,
  1501→            total_tokens=exec_result.total_tokens,
  1502→            total_duration_ms=exec_result.total_duration_ms,
  1503→        )
  1504→    except Exception as exc:
  1505→        logger.exception("Skill execution failed: %s", skill_name)
  1506→        return SkillExecuteResponse(
  1507→            skill_name=skill_name,
  1508→            agent_class=class_name,
  1509→            result=str(exc),
  1510→            status="error",
  1511→        )
  1512→
  1513→
  1514→@app.post("/query/find-capable-agent", response_model=CapabilityQueryResponse)
  1515→async def find_capable_agent(request: CapabilityQueryRequest):
  1516→    """Use CapabilityRegistry to find best agents for a query."""
  1517→    from cohezion.registry.capability_registry import CapabilityRegistry
  1518→
  1519→    registry = CapabilityRegistry()
  1520→    results = registry.find(request.query, top_k=request.top_k)
  1521→    return CapabilityQueryResponse(
  1522→        query=request.query,
  1523→        agents=[
  1524→            {
  1525→                "name": cap.name,
  1526→                "type": cap.type,
  1527→                "description": cap.description,
  1528→                "score": round(cap.score, 4),
  1529→                "path": cap.path,
  1530→            }
  1531→            for cap in results
  1532→        ],
  1533→    )
  1534→

  1539→    from cohezion.agents.factory import AgentFactory
  1540→
  1541→    factory = AgentFactory()
  1542→    names = factory.list_available_skills()
  1543→    return {"count": len(names), "skills": names}

  1600→@app.get("/metrics/agents", response_model=AgentMetricsResponse)
  1601→async def metrics_agents():
  1602→    """Return registered agent stats from CapabilityRegistry."""
  1603→    from cohezion.registry.capability_registry import CapabilityRegistry
  1604→
  1605→    registry = CapabilityRegistry()
  1606→    caps = registry.find("agent", top_k=50)
  1607→    agents = [
  1608→        AgentMetrics(
  1609→            name=cap.name,
  1610→            type=cap.type,
  1611→            description=cap.description,
  1612→            metrics={"score": round(cap.score, 4), "path": cap.path},
  1613→        )
  1614→        for cap in caps
  1615→    ]
  1616→    return AgentMetricsResponse(count=len(agents), agents=agents)
  1617→
  1618→
  1619→@app.get("/metrics/training", response_model=TrainingMetricsResponse)
  1620→async def metrics_training():
  1621→    """Return training metrics from checkpoint files."""
  1622→    import json as _json
  1623→
  1624→    flume_info: dict[str, Any] = {"status": "no_checkpoint"}
  1625→    rl_info: dict[str, Any] = {"status": "no_checkpoint"}
  1626→
  1627→    # FLUME VAE
  1628→    flume_metrics = Path("data/flume/checkpoints/training_metrics.json")
  1629→    flume_ckpt = Path("data/flume/checkpoints/flume_vae_ep50.pt")
  1630→    if flume_metrics.exists():
  1631→        try:
  1632→            data = _json.loads(flume_metrics.read_text())
  1633→            flume_info = {
  1634→                "status": "trained",
  1635→                "epochs": len(data)
  1636→                if isinstance(data, list)
  1637→                else data.get("epochs", 0),
  1638→                "checkpoint": str(flume_ckpt) if flume_ckpt.exists() else None,
  1639→                "metrics": data
  1640→                if isinstance(data, dict)
  1641→                else {"epoch_data": data[-3:]},
  1642→            }
  1643→        except Exception:
  1644→            flume_info = {"status": "checkpoint_found", "path": str(flume_metrics)}
  1645→    elif flume_ckpt.exists():
  1646→        flume_info = {"status": "checkpoint_found", "path": str(flume_ckpt)}
  1647→
  1648→    # RL Policy
  1649→    rl_metrics = Path("data/rl/checkpoints/training_metrics.json")
  1650→    rl_ckpt = Path("data/rl/checkpoints/policy_final.pt")
  1651→    if rl_metrics.exists():
  1652→        try:
  1653→            data = _json.loads(rl_metrics.read_text())
  1654→            rl_info = {
  1655→                "status": "trained",
  1656→                "episodes": len(data)
  1657→                if isinstance(data, list)
  1658→                else data.get("episodes", 0),
  1659→                "checkpoint": str(rl_ckpt) if rl_ckpt.exists() else None,
  1660→                "metrics": data
  1661→                if isinstance(data, dict)
  1662→                else {"episode_data": data[-3:]},
  1663→            }
  1664→        except Exception:
  1665→            rl_info = {"status": "checkpoint_found", "path": str(rl_metrics)}
  1666→    elif rl_ckpt.exists():
  1667→        rl_info = {"status": "checkpoint_found", "path": str(rl_ckpt)}
  1668→
  1669→    return TrainingMetricsResponse(flume_vae=flume_info, rl_policy=rl_info)
  1670→
  1671→
  1672→@app.get("/metrics/pipeline", response_model=PipelineStatusResponse)
  1673→async def metrics_pipeline():
  1674→    """Return pipeline stage completion status."""
  1675→    stages: list[PipelineStageStatus] = []
  1676→
  1677→    # Stage 1: Mass sim .npy export
  1678→    npy_dir = Path("data/mass_sim/artifacts")
  1679→    npy_files = list(npy_dir.glob("*.npy")) if npy_dir.exists() else []
  1680→    stages.append(
  1681→        PipelineStageStatus(
  1682→            stage="mass_sim_export",
  1683→            status="complete" if npy_files else "pending",
  1684→            detail=f"{len(npy_files)} .npy files"
  1685→            if npy_files
  1686→            else "No .npy exports found",
  1687→        )
  1688→    )
  1689→
  1690→    # Stage 2: VAE training
  1691→    vae_ckpt = Path("data/flume/checkpoints/flume_vae_ep50.pt")
  1692→    stages.append(
  1693→        PipelineStageStatus(
  1694→            stage="vae_training",
  1695→            status="complete" if vae_ckpt.exists() else "pending",
  1696→            detail=str(vae_ckpt) if vae_ckpt.exists() else "No VAE checkpoint",
  1697→        )
  1698→    )
  1699→
  1700→    # Stage 3: RL training
  1701→    rl_ckpt = Path("data/rl/checkpoints/policy_final.pt")
  1702→    stages.append(
  1703→        PipelineStageStatus(
  1704→            stage="rl_training",
  1705→            status="complete" if rl_ckpt.exists() else "pending",
  1706→            detail=str(rl_ckpt) if rl_ckpt.exists() else "No RL checkpoint",
  1707→        )
  1708→    )
  1709→
  1710→    # Stage 4: Weight bridge
  1711→    # Check if pipeline has been run (script output or validation file)
  1712→    pipeline_ran = Path("data/pipeline_results")
  1713→    stages.append(
  1714→        PipelineStageStatus(
  1715→            stage="weight_bridge",
  1716→            status="complete" if pipeline_ran.exists() else "pending",
  1717→            detail="Weight bridge validated"
  1718→            if pipeline_ran.exists()
  1719→            else "Not yet executed",
  1720→        )
  1721→    )
  1722→
  1723→    complete = sum(1 for s in stages if s.status == "complete")
  1724→    return PipelineStatusResponse(
  1725→        stages=stages, complete_count=complete, total_count=len(stages)
  1726→    )
  1727→
  1728→
  1729→@app.get("/metrics/system", response_model=SystemMetricsResponse)
  1730→async def metrics_system():
  1731→    """Return system resource metrics."""
  1732→    import psutil
  1733→
  1734→    mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
  1735→
  1736→    # Check Ollama availability
  1737→    ollama_available = False
  1738→    ollama_models: list[str] = []
  1739→    try:
  1740→        import httpx as _httpx
  1741→
  1742→        async with _httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=2.0) as client:
  1743→            resp = await client.get("http://localhost:11434/api/tags")
  1744→            if resp.status_code == 200:
  1745→                ollama_available = True
  1746→                models_data = resp.json().get("models", [])
  1747→                ollama_models = [m["name"] for m in models_data]
  1748→    except Exception:
  1749→        pass
  1750→
  1751→    return SystemMetricsResponse(
  1752→        cpu_percent=psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.1),
  1753→        memory_total_gb=round(mem.total / (1024**3), 2),
  1754→        memory_available_gb=round(mem.available / (1024**3), 2),
  1755→        memory_percent=mem.percent,
  1756→        ollama_available=ollama_available,
  1757→        ollama_models=ollama_models,
  1758→    )
  1759→
  1760→
  1761→@app.post("/knowledge/query", response_model=KnowledgeQueryResponse)
  1762→async def knowledge_query(request: KnowledgeQueryRequest):
  1763→    """Search the knowledge graph for relevant entries."""
  1764→    from cohezion.knowledge_graph.query_engine import KnowledgeGraphQueryEngine
  1765→
  1766→    engine = KnowledgeGraphQueryEngine()
  1767→    results = engine.search_knowledge(request.query, top_k=request.top_k)
  1768→    return KnowledgeQueryResponse(
  1769→        query=request.query, results=results, count=len(results)
  1770→    )
  1771→

  1773→# --- Token Efficiency Metrics ---
  1774→
  1775→
  1776→class TokenMetricsResponse(BaseModel):
  1777→    cache_hits: int = 0
  1778→    cache_misses: int = 0
  1779→    cache_hit_rate: float = 0.0
  1780→    tokens_saved: int = 0
  1781→    total_calls: int = 0
  1782→    model_usage: dict[str, int] = {}
  1783→
  1784→
  1785→@app.get("/metrics/tokens", response_model=TokenMetricsResponse)
  1786→async def metrics_tokens():
  1787→    """Return token efficiency metrics from the shared compound client."""
  1788→    from cohezion.swarm.compound_client import get_compound_client
  1789→
  1790→    # Use a module-level override if set, otherwise the compound singleton
  1791→    client = getattr(metrics_tokens, "_client", None)
  1792→    if client is None:
  1793→        client = get_compound_client()
  1794→    return TokenMetricsResponse(**client.get_metrics())
  1795→
  1796→
  1797→def set_token_client(client: Any) -> None:
  1798→    """Register a TokenEfficientClient for the /metrics/tokens endpoint.
  1799→
  1800→    Pass ``None`` to revert to the default compound client singleton.
  1801→    """
  1802→    metrics_tokens._client = client  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
  1803→
  1804→
  1805→# --- Swarm Execution ---
  1806→
  1807→
  1808→class SwarmExecuteRequest(BaseModel):
  1809→    intent: str
  1810→    max_agents: int = 4
  1811→
  1812→
  1813→class SwarmTaskResult(BaseModel):
  1814→    task_id: str = ""
  1815→    subject: str = ""
  1816→    status: str = ""
  1817→    error: str | None = None
  1818→    duration_ms: float = 0.0
  1819→    tokens: int = 0
  1820→
  1821→
  1822→class SwarmExecuteResponse(BaseModel):
  1823→    report_id: str = ""
  1824→    plan_name: str = ""
  1825→    intent: str = ""
  1826→    status: str = ""
  1827→    total_tokens: int = 0
  1828→    total_duration_ms: float = 0.0
  1829→    tasks: list[SwarmTaskResult] = []
  1830→
  1831→
  1832→@app.post("/swarm/execute", response_model=SwarmExecuteResponse)
  1833→async def swarm_execute(request: SwarmExecuteRequest):
  1834→    """Plan and execute a swarm from a natural language intent."""
  1835→    from cohezion.swarm.compound_client import get_compound_client
  1836→    from cohezion.swarm.execution_orchestrator import ExecutionOrchestrator
  1837→    from cohezion.swarm.team_orchestrator import TeamOrchestrator
  1838→
  1839→    compound = get_compound_client()
  1840→    orchestrator_obj = TeamOrchestrator()
  1841→    plan = orchestrator_obj.plan_team(request.intent, max_agents=request.max_agents)
  1842→    executor = ExecutionOrchestrator(token_client=compound)
  1843→    report = await executor.execute(plan)
  1844→
  1845→    report_dict = report.to_dict()
  1846→    return SwarmExecuteResponse(
  1847→        report_id=report_dict.get("report_id", ""),
  1848→        plan_name=report_dict.get("plan_name", ""),
  1849→        intent=report_dict.get("intent", ""),
  1850→        status=report_dict.get("status", ""),
  1851→        total_tokens=report_dict.get("total_tokens", 0),
  1852→        total_duration_ms=report_dict.get("total_duration_ms", 0.0),
  1853→        tasks=[SwarmTaskResult(**t) for t in report_dict.get("tasks", [])],
  1854→    )
  1855→
  1856→
  1857→# --- Compound Engineering Metrics ---
  1858→
  1859→
  1860→class CompoundMetricsResponse(BaseModel):
  1861→    total_learnings: int = 0
  1862→    top_compound_scores: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1863→    suggested_refinements: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1864→    total_executions: int = 0
  1865→
  1866→
  1867→@app.get("/metrics/compound", response_model=CompoundMetricsResponse)
  1868→async def metrics_compound():
  1869→    """Return compound engineering metrics from retrospection analysis."""
  1870→    from cohezion.compound.metrics import get_collector
  1871→    from cohezion.core.compound.retrospection import RetrospectionEngine
  1872→
  1873→    engine = RetrospectionEngine()
  1874→    learnings = engine.analyze_learnings()
  1875→    scores = engine.calculate_compound_scores()
  1876→    refinements = engine.suggest_skill_refinements()
  1877→
  1878→    top_scores = sorted(scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:10]
  1879→    collector = get_collector()
  1880→
  1881→    return CompoundMetricsResponse(
  1882→        total_learnings=len(learnings),
  1883→        top_compound_scores=[
  1884→            {"name": name, "score": score} for name, score in top_scores
  1885→        ],
  1886→        suggested_refinements=[
  1887→            {
  1888→                "skill_name": r.skill_name,
  1889→                "reason": r.reason,
  1890→                "learning_count": len(r.suggested_additions),
  1891→            }
  1892→            for r in refinements
  1893→        ],
  1894→        total_executions=collector.total_executions,
  1895→    )
  1896→
  1897→
  1898→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1899→# Compound Execution & Feedback endpoints
  1900→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1901→
  1902→
  1903→class CompoundExecuteRequest(BaseModel):
  1904→    skill_name: str
  1905→    input_text: str
  1906→    model: str | None = None
  1907→
  1908→
  1909→class CompoundStepOut(BaseModel):
  1910→    step_index: int
  1911→    operation: str
  1912→    description: str
  1913→    output: str
  1914→    tokens_used: int
  1915→    duration_ms: float
  1916→    model: str = ""
  1917→
  1918→
  1919→class CompoundExecuteResponse(BaseModel):
  1920→    skill_name: str
  1921→    final_output: str
  1922→    steps: list[CompoundStepOut] = []
  1923→    total_tokens: int = 0
  1924→    total_duration_ms: float = 0.0
  1925→    model_usage: dict[str, int] = {}
  1926→
  1927→
  1928→@app.post("/compound/execute", response_model=CompoundExecuteResponse)
  1929→async def compound_execute(request: CompoundExecuteRequest):
  1930→    """Execute a PRIME skill with live Ollama models via CompoundExecutor."""
  1931→    from cohezion.compound.executor import get_executor
  1932→
  1933→    executor = get_executor()
  1934→    try:
  1935→        result = await executor.execute_skill(
  1936→            request.skill_name,
  1937→            request.input_text,
  1938→            model=request.model,
  1939→        )
  1940→    except KeyError as exc:
  1941→        raise HTTPException(
  1942→            status_code=404, detail=f"Skill not found: {request.skill_name}"
  1943→        ) from exc
  1944→    except Exception as exc:
  1945→        logger.exception("Compound execution failed: %s", request.skill_name)
  1946→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc)) from exc
  1947→
  1948→    return CompoundExecuteResponse(
  1949→        skill_name=result.skill_name,
  1950→        final_output=result.final_output,
  1951→        steps=[
  1952→            CompoundStepOut(
  1953→                step_index=s["step_index"],
  1954→                operation=s["operation"],
  1955→                description=s["description"],
  1956→                output=s["output"],
  1957→                tokens_used=s["tokens_used"],
  1958→                duration_ms=s["duration_ms"],
  1959→                model=s.get("model", ""),
  1960→            )
  1961→            for s in result.steps
  1962→        ],
  1963→        total_tokens=result.total_tokens,
  1964→        total_duration_ms=result.total_duration_ms,
  1965→        model_usage=result.model_usage,
  1966→    )
  1967→
  1968→
  1969→class CompoundFeedbackRequest(BaseModel):
  1970→    skill_name: str
  1971→    input_text: str
  1972→    model: str | None = None
  1973→    cycles: int = 1
  1974→
  1975→
  1976→class CompoundFeedbackResponse(BaseModel):
  1977→    skill_name: str
  1978→    cycles_completed: int = 0
  1979→    total_tokens: int = 0
  1980→    total_duration_ms: float = 0.0
  1981→    total_refinements: int = 0
  1982→    compound_score_delta: float = 0.0
  1983→    patterns: list[str] = []
  1984→
  1985→
  1986→@app.post("/compound/feedback", response_model=CompoundFeedbackResponse)
  1987→async def compound_feedback(request: CompoundFeedbackRequest):
  1988→    """Run a compound feedback cycle: execute -> analyze -> refine."""
  1989→    from cohezion.compound.feedback_loop import CompoundFeedbackLoop
  1990→
  1991→    loop = CompoundFeedbackLoop()
  1992→    try:
  1993→        if request.cycles > 1:
  1994→            report = await loop.run_multi_cycle(
  1995→                request.skill_name,
  1996→                request.input_text,
  1997→                cycles=request.cycles,
  1998→                model=request.model,
  1999→            )
  2000→            return CompoundFeedbackResponse(
  2001→                skill_name=report.skill_name,
  2002→                cycles_completed=report.total_cycles,
  2003→                total_tokens=report.total_tokens,
  2004→                total_duration_ms=report.total_duration_ms,
  2005→                total_refinements=report.total_refinements,
  2006→                compound_score_delta=report.final_compound_score_delta,
  2007→            )
  2008→        else:
  2009→            result = await loop.run_cycle(
  2010→                request.skill_name,
  2011→                request.input_text,
  2012→                model=request.model,
  2013→            )
  2014→            return CompoundFeedbackResponse(
  2015→                skill_name=result.skill_name,
  2016→                cycles_completed=1,
  2017→                total_tokens=result.execution_tokens,
  2018→                total_duration_ms=result.execution_duration_ms,
  2019→                total_refinements=result.refinements_applied,
  2020→                compound_score_delta=result.compound_score_delta,
  2021→                patterns=result.patterns,
  2022→            )
  2023→    except KeyError as exc:
  2024→        raise HTTPException(
  2025→            status_code=404, detail=f"Skill not found: {request.skill_name}"
  2026→        ) from exc
  2027→    except Exception as exc:
  2028→        logger.exception("Compound feedback failed: %s", request.skill_name)
  2029→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc)) from exc
  2030→
  2031→
  2032→class CompoundHealthResponse(BaseModel):
  2033→    total_executions: int = 0

  2035→    total_cycles: int = 0

  2040→    compound_score_trend: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  2041→
  2042→
  2043→@app.get("/compound/health", response_model=CompoundHealthResponse)

  2052→class CompoundHistoryResponse(BaseModel):
