`CompoundFeedbackRequest`, `CompoundFeedbackResponse`\n- `CompoundHealthResponse`, `CompoundHistoryResponse`\n\n### Step 3: Create Route Modules\n\n**Target**: `src/cohezion/api/routes/`\n\n```\nroutes/__init__.py → Re-export all routers\nroutes/core.py → health, mcp endpoints\nroutes/knowledge.py → knowledge search, skills\nroutes/swarm.py → swarm debate, perspectives, metrics\nroutes/notebooks.py → notebook CRUD\nroutes/simulations.py → simulation CRUD\nroutes/journeys.py → journey visualization (250 lines ⭐)\nroutes/flume.py → VAE training (200 lines)\nroutes/templates.py → template parsing\nroutes/rl.py → RL training, inference (250 lines ⭐)\nroutes/skills.py → skill execution, capabilities\nroutes/metrics.py → observability (300 lines ⭐)\nroutes/compound.py → compound execution, feedback\n```\n\n**Pattern for each module**:\n```python\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException\nfrom cohezion.api.models import DebateRequest, DebateResponse\nfrom cohezion.mcp.swarm_server import get_server as get_swarm_server\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/swarm\", tags=[\"swarm\"])\n\n@router.post(\"/debate\", response_model=DebateResponse)\nasync def run_debate(request: DebateRequest):\n    # ...\n\n# Export\n__all__ = [\"router\"]\n```\n\n### Step 4: Refactor __init__.py\n\n```python\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\nfrom fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Import all routers\nfrom cohezion.api.routes import (\n    core_router,\n    knowledge_router,\n    swarm_router,\n    notebooks_router,\n    simulations_router,\n    journeys_router,\n    flume_router,\n    templates_router,\n    rl_router,\n    skills_router,\n    metrics_router,\n    compound_router,\n)\n\napp = FastAPI(\n    title=\"Cohezion API\",\n    description=\"AI Research Lab API\",\n    version=\"0.1.0\",\n)\n\napp.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, ...)\napp.mount(\"/static\", ...)\n\n@app.get(\"/\")\nasync def root():\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/static/index.html\")\n\n# Include all routers\napp.include_router(core_router)\napp.include_router(knowledge_router)\n# ... include all routers\n\n__all__ = [\"app\"]\n```\n\n### Step 5: Verify Tests Pass\n\n```bash\nuv run pytest tests/api/test_route_modularization.py -q --tb=short\n\nExpected: All tests pass ✅\n- 40+ endpoints registered\n- All prefixes found\n- 30+ OpenAPI schemas\n- GET, POST methods present\n```\n\n### Step 6: Run Full API Sanity Check\n\n```bash\n# Start API\nuv run uvicorn cohezion.api:app --reload &\n\n# Test key endpoints\ncurl http://localhost:8000/health\ncurl http://localhost:8000/flume/status\ncurl http://localhost:8000/rl/policy-info\n```\n\n### Step 7: Commit & Push\n\n```bash\ngit add -A\ngit commit -m \"refactor: modularize API routes into cohesive route files\n\nExtracts 2,074-line monolithic __init__.py into 12 focused route modules:\n- core (health, MCP registry)\n- knowledge (search, skills)\n- swarm (debate, perspectives)\n- notebooks (CRUD)\n- simulations (CRUD)\n- journeys (visualization, trajectories)\n- flume (VAE training, encode/decode)\n- templates (template parsing)\n- rl (training, inference, episodes)\n- skills (execution, capabilities)\n- metrics (observability, system metrics)\n- compound (execution, feedback, health)\n\nShared models extracted to api/models.py for reusability.\n\nBenefits:\n- __init__.py reduced from 2,074 → ~100 lines\n- Clear separation of concerns\n- Easier to test and maintain\n- Better for future extensions\n- No behavioral changes (tests unchanged)\n\nSee PHASE_1_PLAN.md for architecture details.\"\n\ngit push origin spec/routes-consolidation\n```\n\n## Testing Strategy\n\n### Baseline Tests (Run First)\n- Route count (40+)\n- Route prefixes\n- Pydantic models (30+)\n- HTTP methods\n\n### Sanity Tests (Run After)\n- Health endpoint works\n- FLUME endpoints accessible\n- RL endpoints accessible\n- Metrics endpoints accessible\n\n## Critical Notes\n\n1. **NO behavioral changes** - Just reorganization\n2. **Extract models FIRST** - Prevents import errors\n3. **Test FREQUENTLY** - After each route module\n4. **Use APIRouter** - Not creating independent apps\n5. **Import from models** - All Pydantic classes from api.models\n\n## Risks & Mitigations\n\n| Risk | Mitigation |\n|------|-----------|\n| Import cycles | models.py has NO imports from routes/ |\n| Missing endpoints | Baseline test ensures all 40+ present |\n| Broken response models | Extract to shared models.py first |\n| Missing middleware | Keep middleware in __init__.py |\n| Broken static files | Mount in __init__.py after includes |\n\n## Success Criteria\n\n- ✅ Tests pass (test_route_modularization.py)\n- ✅ __init__.py < 150 lines\n- ✅ 12 route modules created\n- ✅ models.py with 15+ classes\n- ✅ No endpoints lost\n- ✅ API starts without errors\n- ✅ Ready for Phase 2 (Executor Formalization)\n\n## Token Accounting\n\n- Models extraction: 100 tokens\n- Routes creation (12 files): 700 tokens\n- __init__.py refactor: 150 tokens\n- Tests & verification: 150 tokens\n- **Total: ~1,100 tokens (under 1,500 budget)**\n\n## Next Phase (Phase 2)\n\nAfter Phase 1 complete:\n- Executor Formalization (executor_steps.py integration)\n- Formal step typing and journeys tracking\n- RetrospectionEngine updates\n- Tests: executor behavior, journey tracking\n\n---\n\n**Branch**: spec/routes-consolidation\n**Approved**: Yes\n**Ready for Implementation**: Yes\n**Session**: Next session, run: `git checkout spec/routes-consolidation && git pull`\n"
}

    36→class CompoundExecutor:
    37→    """Executor for compound engineering tasks with vault integration.
    38→
    39→    Lifecycle:
    40→      1. get_experience_guidance() - Query vault for similar tasks
    41→      2. execute_task() - Run the task with token-efficient client
    42→      3. Logs are persisted to vault automatically
    43→      4. extract_patterns() - Save reusable insights
    44→
    45→    Optionally uses TokenEfficientClient for token-efficient LLM operations:
    46→      - SHA-256 caching eliminates redundant API calls
    47→      - Batch processing (Phase 1 cache + Phase 2 parallel) saves tokens
    48→      - Token metrics captured in ExecutionResult for compound scoring
    49→    """
    50→
    51→    def __init__(
    52→        self,
    53→        mcp_client: MCPClient,
    54→        token_client: Any | None = None,
    55→        guardrail_pipeline: GuardrailPipeline | None = None,
    56→        enable_guardrails: bool = True,
    57→        inflection_detector: Any | None = None,
    58→        skill_refiner: Any | None = None,
    59→        enable_skill_refinement: bool = True,
    60→        metrics_collector: Any | None = None,
    61→        journey_tracker: Any | None = None,
    62→        journey_persistence: Any | None = None,
    63→        alignment_analyzer: Any | None = None,
    64→        enable_alignment_analysis: bool = False,
    65→        degradation_detector: Any | None = None,
    66→        model_quality_classifier: Any | None = None,
    67→        retrospection_engine: Any | None = None,
    68→        universe_bridge: Any | None = None,
    69→    ):
    70→        """Initialize compound executor.
    71→
    72→        Args:
    73→            mcp_client: Connected MCPClient for vault operations
    74→            token_client: Optional TokenEfficientClient for LLM operations
    75→                If provided, enables token-efficient caching and batching
    76→            guardrail_pipeline: Optional GuardrailPipeline for safety checks.
    77→                If None and enable_guardrails is True, creates default pipeline.
    78→                If None and enable_guardrails is False, no guardrails applied.
    79→            enable_guardrails: If True (default), enable guardrails via default
    80→                pipeline (unless guardrail_pipeline is provided).
    81→            inflection_detector: Optional InflectionDetector for anomaly detection.
    82→                If None, creates default detector automatically.
    83→            skill_refiner: Optional SkillRefiner for learning from executions.
    84→                If None and enable_skill_refinement is True, creates default.
    85→            enable_skill_refinement: If True (default), enable skill refinement.
    86→            metrics_collector: Optional CompoundMetricsCollector for recording
    87→                execution metrics. If None, no metrics recorded.
    88→            journey_tracker: Optional JourneyTracker for 12D FLUME trajectory
    89→                tracking. If None, no journey tracking.
    90→            journey_persistence: Optional JourneyPersistence for persisting
    91→                journey data. Requires journey_tracker to be set.
    92→            alignment_analyzer: Optional RequestAlignmentAnalyzer for request
    93→                alignment analysis. If None and enable_alignment_analysis is True,
    94→                creates default analyzer.
    95→            enable_alignment_analysis: If True, enable alignment analysis
    96→                (requires alignment_analyzer or auto-creates one).
    97→            degradation_detector: Optional DegradationDetector for monitoring
    98→                metric drops (cache hit rate, token efficiency, coherence).
    99→                If None, no degradation detection.
   100→            model_quality_classifier: Optional ModelQualityClassifier for

     1→"""Compound engineering endpoints - execute, feedback, health, history."""
     2→
     3→import logging
     4→
     5→from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException
     6→
     7→from cohezion.api.models import (
     8→    CompoundExecuteRequest,
     9→    CompoundExecuteResponse,
    10→    CompoundFeedbackRequest,
    11→    CompoundFeedbackResponse,
    12→    CompoundHealthResponse,
    13→    CompoundHistoryResponse,
    14→    CompoundStepOut,
    15→)
    16→
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→router = APIRouter(prefix="/compound", tags=["compound"])
    21→
    22→
    23→@router.post("/execute", response_model=CompoundExecuteResponse)
    24→async def compound_execute(request: CompoundExecuteRequest):
    25→    """Execute a PRIME skill with live Ollama models via CompoundExecutor."""
    26→    from cohezion.compound.executor import get_executor  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    27→
    28→    executor = get_executor()  # type: ignore[reportAttributeAccessIssue]
    29→    try:
    30→        result = await executor.execute_skill(  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    31→            request.skill_name,
    32→            request.input_text,
    33→            model=request.model,
    34→        )
    35→    except KeyError as exc:
    36→        raise HTTPException(
    37→            status_code=404, detail=f"Skill not found: {request.skill_name}"
    38→        ) from exc
    39→    except Exception as exc:
    40→        logger.exception("Compound execution failed: %s", request.skill_name)
    41→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    42→
    43→    return CompoundExecuteResponse(
    44→        skill_name=result.skill_name,
    45→        final_output=result.final_output,
    46→        steps=[
    47→            CompoundStepOut(
    48→                step_index=s["step_index"],
    49→                operation=s["operation"],
    50→                description=s["description"],
    51→                output=s["output"],
    52→                tokens_used=s["tokens_used"],
    53→                duration_ms=s["duration_ms"],
    54→                model=s.get("model", ""),
    55→            )
    56→            for s in result.steps
    57→        ],
    58→        total_tokens=result.total_tokens,
    59→        total_duration_ms=result.total_duration_ms,
    60→        model_usage=result.model_usage,
    61→    )
    62→
    63→
    64→@router.post("/feedback", response_model=CompoundFeedbackResponse)
    65→async def compound_feedback(request: CompoundFeedbackRequest):
    66→    """Run a compound feedback cycle: execute -> analyze -> refine."""
    67→    from cohezion.compound.feedback_loop import CompoundFeedbackLoop
    68→
    69→    loop = CompoundFeedbackLoop()  # type: ignore[reportCallIssue]
    70→    try:
    71→        if request.cycles > 1:
    72→            report = await loop.run_multi_cycle(  # type: ignore[reportAttributeAccessIssue]
    73→                request.skill_name,
    74→                request.input_text,
    75→                cycles=request.cycles,
    76→                model=request.model,
    77→            )
    78→            return CompoundFeedbackResponse(
    79→                skill_name=report.skill_name,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    80→                cycles_completed=report.total_cycles,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    81→                total_tokens=report.total_tokens,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    82→                total_duration_ms=report.total_duration_ms,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    83→                total_refinements=report.total_refinements,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    84→                compound_score_delta=report.final_compound_score_delta,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    85→            )
    86→        else:
    87→            result = await loop.run_cycle(  # type: ignore[reportAttributeAccessIssue]
    88→                request.skill_name,
    89→                request.input_text,
    90→                model=request.model,
    91→            )
    92→            return CompoundFeedbackResponse(
    93→                skill_name=result.skill_name,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    94→                cycles_completed=1,
    95→                total_tokens=result.execution_tokens,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    96→                total_duration_ms=result.execution_duration_ms,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    97→                total_refinements=result.refinements_applied,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    98→                compound_score_delta=result.compound_score_delta,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
    99→                patterns=result.patterns,  # type: ignore[reportUnknownMemberType]
   100→            )
   101→    except KeyError as exc:
   102→        raise HTTPException(
   103→            status_code=404, detail=f"Skill not found: {request.skill_name}"
   104→        ) from exc
   105→    except Exception as exc:
   106→        logger.exception("Compound feedback failed: %s", request.skill_name)
   107→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(exc)) from exc
   108→
   109→
   110→@router.get("/health", response_model=CompoundHealthResponse)
   111→async def compound_health():
   112→    """Return compound system health from the metrics collector."""
   113→    from cohezion.compound.metrics import get_collector
   114→
   115→    collector = get_collector()
   116→    return CompoundHealthResponse(**collector.to_health_dict())
   117→
   118→
   119→@router.get("/history/{skill_name}", response_model=CompoundHistoryResponse)
   120→async def compound_history(skill_name: str):
   121→    """Return compound execution history for a specific skill."""
   122→    from cohezion.compound.metrics import get_collector
   123→
   124→    collector = get_collector()
   125→    history = collector.get_skill_history(skill_name)  # type: ignore[reportAttributeAccessIssue]
   126→    return CompoundHistoryResponse(skill_name=skill_name, executions=history)
   127→

        """Get or create singleton executor.

        Args:
            mcp_client: Connected MCP client
            token_client: Optional TokenEfficientClient for token-efficient operations
            guardrail_pipeline: Optional GuardrailPipeline for safety checks
            enable_guardrails: If True (default), enable guardrails
            inflection_detector: Optional InflectionDetector for anomaly detection
            skill_refiner: Optional SkillRefiner for learning from executions
            enable_skill_refinement: If True (default), enable skill refinement
            metrics_collector: Optional CompoundMetricsCollector
            journey_tracker: Optional JourneyTracker
            journey_persistence: Optional JourneyPersistence
            alignment_analyzer: Optional RequestAlignmentAnalyzer
            enable_alignment_analysis: If True, enable alignment analysis
            degradation_detector: Optional DegradationDetector
            model_quality_classifier: Optional ModelQualityClassifier
            retrospection_engine: Optional RetrospectionEngine
            universe_bridge: Optional UniverseBridge

        Returns:
            Singleton CompoundExecutor instance
        """
        if ExecutorFactory._instance is None:
            ExecutorFactory._instance = CompoundExecutor(
                mcp_client,
                token_client,
                guardrail_pipeline,
                enable_guardrails,
                inflection_detector,
                skill_refiner,
                enable_skill_refinement,
                metrics_collector=metrics_collector,
                journey_tracker=journey_tracker,
                journey_persistence=journey_persistence,
                alignment_analyzer=alignment_analyzer,
                enable_alignment_analysis=enable_alignment_analysis,
                degradation_detector=degradation_detector,
                model_quality_classifier=model_quality_classifier,
                retrospection_engine=retrospection_engine,
                universe_bridge=universe_bridge,
            )
        return ExecutorFactory._instance

class MCPClientError(Exception):
    """Base exception for MCP client errors."""


class MCPConnectionError(MCPClientError):
    """Connection to MCP server failed."""


class MCPAuthenticationError(MCPClientError):
    """Authentication with MCP server failed."""

--
class MCPClient:
    """Client for Cloud Vault MCP Server operations.

    Provides methods to interact with the Cloud Vault MCP Server for:
    - Vault operations (read, write, edit, delete, search)
    - Obsidian operations (backlinks, forward links, tags, templates)
    - Compound operations (decisions, experiments, patterns, context)

executor gracefully degrades if vault unavailable.\n\n    Returns:\n        Singleton CompoundExecutor instance\n\n    Raises:\n        RuntimeError: If core initialization fails\n    \"\"\"\n    import os\n\n    global _executor_instance\n\n    if _executor_instance is not None:\n        return _executor_instance\n\n    try:\n        from cohezion.core.mcp_client import MCPConfig\n\n        # Try to initialize vault client from environment\n        # Gracefully degrade if vault not configured\n        mcp_client = None\n        try:\n            server_url = os.getenv(\"MCP_SERVER_URL\", \"ws://localhost:8000\")\n            api_key = os.getenv(\"MCP_API_KEY\", \"\")\n            config = MCPConfig(\n                server_url=server_url,\n                api_key=api_key,\n                timeout=30.0,\n                max_retries=3,\n            )\n            mcp_client = MCPClient(config)\n            logger.info(\"Initialized MCP client for compound executor\")\n        except Exception as vault_exc:\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Vault integration unavailable, executor will work in degraded mode: %s\",\n                vault_exc,\n            )\n            # Continue without vault - <tool_use_error>String to replace not found in file.
String: # Module-level singleton for API access
_executor_instance: CompoundExecutor | None = None


def get_executor() -> CompoundExecutor:
    """Get or create singleton executor for API access.

    Lazily initializes CompoundExecutor with minimal vault integration.
    For full customization, use ExecutorFactory.create() with explicit params.

    Returns:
        Singleton CompoundExecutor instance

    Raises:
        RuntimeError: If MCP client initialization fails
    """
    global _executor_instance

    if _executor_instance is not None:
        return _executor_instance

    try:
        # Initialize minimal vault client
        mcp_client = MCPClient()

        # Create executor with standard configuration
        _executor_instance = CompoundExecutor(
            mcp_client=mcp_client,
            enable_guardrails=True,
            enable_skill_refinement=True,
            enable_alignment_analysis=False,  # Skip for API performance
        )
        logger.info("Initialized compound executor singleton for API access")
        return _executor_instance

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("Failed to initialize compound executor: %s", exc)
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Compound executor initialization failed: {exc}"
        ) from exc</tool_use_error>

   915→    def _compute_token_delta(
   916→        self,
   917→        metrics_before: dict[str, Any] | None,
   918→        metrics_after: dict[str, Any],
   919→    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   920→        """Compute token metric deltas (tokens used in this execution).
   921→
   922→        Args:
   923→            metrics_before: Token metrics before execution
   924→            metrics_after: Token metrics after execution
   925→
   926→        Returns:
   927→            Dict with token delta information

   929→        if not metrics_before:
   930→            # First execution, can't compute delta
   931→            return metrics_after
   932→
   933→        delta: dict[str, Any] = {}
   934→
   935→        # Compute differences
   936→        if "total_tokens" in metrics_after and "total_tokens" in metrics_before:
   937→            delta["tokens_used"] = (
   938→                metrics_after["total_tokens"] - metrics_before["total_tokens"]
   939→            )
   940→        if "api_calls" in metrics_after and "api_calls" in metrics_before:
   941→            delta["api_calls_made"] = (
   942→                metrics_after["api_calls"] - metrics_before["api_calls"]
   943→            )
   944→        if "cache_hits" in metrics_after and "cache_hits" in metrics_before:
   945→            delta["cache_hits"] = metrics_after["cache_hits"] - metrics_before["cache_hits"]
   946→        if "cache_misses" in metrics_after and "cache_misses" in metrics_before:
   947→            delta["cache_misses"] = (
   948→                metrics_after["cache_misses"] - metrics_before["cache_misses"]
   949→            )
   950→
   951→        # Include final hit rate
   952→        if "cache_hit_rate" in metrics_after:
   953→            delta["cache_hit_rate"] = metrics_after["cache_hit_rate"]
   954→
   955→        # Pass through model name and other non-delta fields
   956→        if "model" in metrics_after:
   957→            delta["model"] = metrics_after["model"]
   958→        if "combined_hit_rate" in metrics_after:
   959→            delta["combined_hit_rate"] = metrics_after["combined_hit_rate"]
   960→        if "tokens_per_second" in metrics_after:
   961→            delta["tokens_per_second"] = metrics_after["tokens_per_second"]
   962→
   963→        return delta
